AADMINISTRATIVEREGULATIONS
A1COMPETITIONOVERVIEW
A1.1CompetitionObjective
A1.1.1
Thecompetitionchallengesteamsofuniversitystudentstoconceive,design,fabricate,developandcompetewithsmallformulastyleracecars.
A1.2CompetitionProcedure
A1.2.1
Thecompetitionissplitintothefollowingclasses:
\225
InternalCombustionEngineVehicle\050CV\051includingCombustionHybridVehicle\050HY\051
\225
ElectricVehicle\050EV\051
A1.2.2
Allvehiclesmustmeettherequirementsde\002nedinchaptersTandeitherEVorCV,depend-ingontheirdrivetraintype.
A1.2.3
ThecompetitionstartswithTechnicalInspection,describedinchapterIN,tocheckthevehicleforsafetyandcompliancewiththerules.
A1.2.4
ThecompetitionisdividedintoaseriesofstaticanddynamicdisciplinesdescribedinchaptersSandD.
A1.2.5
Theachievablepointsineachclassarelistedintable3.
A1.2.6
VehiclesofbothclassescantakepartinanadditionalDriverlessCup\050DC\051.
A1.2.7
ThemaximumachievablepointsfortheDCarelistedintable3.
A1.2.8
TheteamwiththemostoverallpointswillwinthecompetitionforitsclassortheDC,respectively.

A1.3.1
Theof\002ciallanguageofthecompetitionisEnglish.

A2.1.1

A2.1.4

A3RulesofConduct
A3RULESOFCONDUCT
A3.1GeneralOf\002cialsAuthority
A3.1.1
Theof\002cialsreservetherighttorevisethescheduleoftheeventand/orinterpretormodifytheeventorcompetitionrulesatanytimeandinanymannerthatis,intheirsolejudgment,requiredforsafeandef\002cientoperation.
A3.1.2
Allteammembersarerequiredtocooperatewith,andfollowallinstructionsfromtheof\002cials.
A3.1.3
Of\002cialannouncementsareconsideredpartoftheserules.
A3.1.4
Allguidelinesandclari\002cationspostedinthe\223RulesandImportantDocuments\224sectionsonthecompetitionwebsiteforthecurrentseasonincludingtheEventHandbookareconsideredpartoftheserules.
A3.2Of\002cialInstructions
A3.2.1
Failureofateammembertofollowaninstructionorcommanddirectedspeci\002callytothatteamorteammemberresultsin25penaltypointsbeingdeductedfromtheteam'soverallscore.
A3.3ArgumentswithOf\002cials
A3.3.1
Argumentwith,ordisobedienceto,anyof\002cialresultsintheteambeingeliminatedfromtheevent.
A3.4UnsportsmanlikeConduct
A3.4.1
Intheeventofunsportsmanlikeconduct,25penaltypointswillbedeductedfromtheteam'soverallscore.Asecondviolationresultsinexpulsionoftheteamfromtheevent.
A3.5ViolationsofIntent
A3.5.1
Violationoftheintentofarulewillbeconsideredaviolationoftheruleitself.
A3.5.2
Anyparts,devicesorsoftwarefragmentsdesignedwiththeintenttoviolatearule,willbeconsideredasaviolation.
A3.6QuestionsabouttheRules
A3.6.1
Questionsabouttherulesmaybeaskedtotheof\002cials.
A3.6.2
Thefrequentlyaskedquestions\050FAQ\051sectiononthecompetitionwebsitemustbecheckedbeforesubmittingaquestion.
A3.6.3
Theof\002cialswillonlyanswerquestionsthatarenotalreadyansweredintherulesorFAQsorthatrequirenewornovelinterpretation.

A4GeneralRequirementsforTeams&Participants
A3.6.4
Refertothecompetitionwebsiteforspeci\002cdirectionshowtosubmitarulesquestion.
A3.7Protests
A3.7.1
Ateammayprotestanyruleinterpretation,scoreorof\002cialactionwhichtheyfeelhascausedsomeactual,non-trivial,harmtotheirteam,orhashadasubstantiveeffectontheirscore.
A3.7.2
Allprotestsmustbesubmittedinwritingasde\002nedintheEventHandbookbytheteamcaptainwithintheannouncedprotestperiod.Inordertohaveaprotestconsidered,ateammustposta25pointprotestbondwhichwillbeforfeitediftheirprotestisrejected.
A3.7.3
Thedecisionoftheof\002cialsregardinganyprotestwillbeinawrittenformandis\002nal.
A3.8Penalties
A3.8.1
Ruleviolationsresultinatleast20penaltypoints,unlessexplicitlyde\002nedotherwise.Thepenaltypointswillbedeductedfromtheteam'soverallscore.
A3.8.2
OverallpenaltiesapplytoCV,EVandDCscore,exceptforpenaltiesconcerningdisciplineswhicharenotpartoftherespectiveclassorcup.

A4.1TeamsperUniversity

T2.8SteeringSystem
T2.8.1
Steeringsystemsusingcablesorbeltsforactuationareprohibited.Thisdoesnotapplyforautonomoussteeringactuators.

Thesteeringwheelmustbenomorethan250mmrearwardofthefronthoop.Thisdistanceismeasuredhorizontally,onthevehiclecenterline,fromtherearsurfaceofthefronthooptotheforwardmostsurfaceofthesteeringwheelwiththesteeringsysteminanyposition.
T2.8.7
Thesteeringwheelmusthaveacontinuousperimeterthatisnearcircularornearoval.Theouterperimeterpro\002lemayhavesomestraightsections,butnoconcavesections.
T2.8.8
Inanyangularposition,thetopofthesteeringwheelmustbenohigherthanthetop-mostsurfaceofthefronthoop.
T2.8.9
Thesteeringrackmustbemechanicallyattachedtotheprimarystructureandallstationarypartsmustbewithintherolloverprotectionenvelope,seeT1.1.16.
T2.8.10
JointsbetweenallcomponentsattachingthesteeringwheeltothesteeringrackmustbemechanicalandvisibleatTechnicalInspection.BondedjointsareallowedinaccordancewithT3.2.8.

Rearwheelsteering,whichcanbeelectricallyactuated,isallowedifmechanicalstopslimittherangeofangularmovementoftherearwheelstoamaximumof6\016.Thismustbe

T3.19ImpactAttenuatorTestRequirements
T3.19.1

T3.19.2

\225

Therepresentativetest\002xtureoftheintendedprimarystructuremaynotbestructurallycompromised\050i.e.nocracks\051afterthetest.
T3.19.3
TeamsusingIAs\050typicallystructuralnoses\051directlyattachedtothefrontbulkhead,whichshortcuttheloadpaththroughthebulkoftheAIP,mustconductanadditionaltest.ThistestmustprovethattheAIPcanwithstandaloadof120kN\050300kgmultipliedby40g\051,wheretheloadapplicatormatchestheminimumIAdimensions.
T3.19.4
Vehicleswithaerodynamicdevicesand/orsensorsforwardofthefrontbulkheadmustnotexceedthepeakdecelerationofT3.19.1forthecombinationoftheirIAassemblyandthenon-crushableobject\050s\051.Oneofthefollowingthreemethodsmustbeusedtoprovethedesigndoesnotexceed120kN:
\225
PhysicaltestingoftheIAassemblyincludinganynon-crushableobject\050s\051orgeo-metricallyrepresentativedummiesthereofforwardoftheAIP.Whennon-crushableobject\050s\051arelocatedinfrontviewoftheAIP,physicaltestingisrequired.

T8AERODYNAMICDEVICES
T8.1De\002nitionAerodynamicDevice
T8.1.1
Aspeci\002callydesignedstructuremountedonthevehicletoguidetheair\003owaroundthevehicle,increasingthedownforceactingonthevehicleand/orloweringitsdrag.Themountingofthisstructureisnotregardedasanaerodynamicdevice,unlessitisintentionallydesignedtobeone.
T8.2RestrictionsforAerodynamicDevices
T8.2.1
Heightrestrictions:
\225
Allaerodynamicdevicesforwardofaverticalplanethroughtherearmostportionofthefrontfaceofthedriverheadrestraintsupport,excludinganypadding,settoitsmostrearwardposition,mustbelowerthan500mmfromtheground.
\225
Allaerodynamicdevicesinfrontofthefrontaxleandextendingfurtheroutboardthanthemostinboardpointofthefronttire/wheelmustbelowerthan250mmfromtheground.

eitherbemountedinproximitytotheASMSoronthetopsideofthevehiclebetweenfrontbulkheadandfronthoopclosetothevehiclescenterline

bemarkedwith\223Brakerelease\224

havearedhandle

CV1.4.1

CV5HybridSystem
CV5.1.6
A\002rewall,seeT4.8,mustbepresentbetweentheHSCandthefueltank.
CV5.1.7
TheHSCmustbepositionedaccordingtoT11.7.2,allotherhybridsystemcomponentsmustbepositionedwithinthesurfaceenvelope,seeT1.1.18.
CV5.1.8
Thehighcurrentpath,seeEV1.2.2,ofthehybridsystemmustmeetEV4.5.15.
CV5.1.9
MotorsmustmeetEV2.1.
CV5.1.10
Thehybridsystemmayonlybeactivatedwhenthecombustionengineisrunningorduringenginestart.
CV5.2HybridStorageContainer
CV5.2.1
TheHSCmustbeattachedtotheprimarystructure,seeT1.1.12,accordingtoT3.3.1.
CV5.2.2
Adisconnectionmechanism,designedasanAIRmustbeintegratedinsideoftheHSC,disconnectingthepositivepoleoftheHSC.TheAIRmustbecompliantwithEV5.6.3.
CV5.2.3
Themaximumcombinedtotalweightofallelementsinthehybridsystemthatstoretheelectricenergy,e.g.batterycellsorsupercapacitors,includingallcasingsandtabsthatareintegraltothem,is3kg.
CV5.2.4
Holes,bothinternalandexternal,intheHSC,areonlyallowedforthewiringharness,ventilation,cooling,orfasteners.Thetotalcutoutareamustbebelow25%oftheareaoftherespectivesinglewall.
CV5.2.5
TheHSCmustberemovabletobeinspectedattheMechanicalInspectionanditmustbepossibletoeasilychecktheweightlimit.
CV5.3HybridSystemForm\050HSF\051
CV5.3.1
AHSFhastobesubmittedusingtheHSFtemplate.
CV5.3.2
TheHSFtemplatewillbeavailableonthecompetitionwebsite.
CV5.3.3
IfnoHSFissubmitted,theteammustnotusethehybridsystemattheevent.A5.4.2willnotbeappliedfortheHSF.

EVELECTRICVEHICLES
EV1DEFINITIONS
EV1.1TractiveSystem
EV1.1.1
TractiveSystem\050TS\051\226everypartthatiselectricallyconnectedtothemotorsandTSaccumulators.TheLVSmaybesuppliedbytheTSifagalvanicisolationbetweenbothsystemsisensured.
EV1.1.2
TSenclosures\226everyhousingorenclosurethatcontainspartsoftheTS.
EV1.2Electrical
EV1.2.1
GalvanicIsolation\226twoelectriccircuitsarede\002nedasgalvanicallyisolatedifallofthefollowingconditionsaretrue:
\225
Theresistancebetweenbothcircuitsis\025500W=V,relatedtothemaximumTSvoltageofthevehicle,atatestvoltageofmaximumTSvoltageor250V,whicheverishigher.
\225
TheisolationtestvoltageRMS,ACfor1min,betweenbothcircuitsishigherthanthreetimesthemaximumTSvoltageor750V,whicheverishigher.
\225
Theworkingvoltageoftheisolationbarrier,ifspeci\002edinthedatasheet,ishigherthanthemaximumTSvoltage.Capacitorsthatbridgegalvanicisolationmustbeclass-Ycapacitors.
EV1.2.2
HighCurrentPath\226anypathofaTScircuitrythat,duringnormaloperation,carriesmorethan1A.

EV3GeneralRequirements
EV2.2.2
TheTScurrentattheoutletoftheTSACmustnotexceed500A.
EV2.2.3
Regeneratingenergyisallowedandunrestrictedwithregardtopower.
EV2.2.4
Wheelsmustnotbespuninreverse.
EV3GENERALREQUIREMENTS
EV3.1Grounding
EV3.1.1
TSenclosures,seeEV1.1.2,mustconsistofeither
\225
agroundedsolidlayermadeofatleast0:5mmthickelectricallyconductivematerial,aluminiumorbetter,seeEV3.1.3or
\225
bemadeoutofelectricallyinsulatingmaterialsprovidinganisolationresistanceofatleast2MW,measuredatavoltageof500V,betweentheinsideandtheoutsideoftheenclosure.TheTSenclosuremustberigidandmustpreventpossiblemechanicalpenetrations.Protrudingelectricallyconductiveparts,suchasfastenersorconnectors,mustfollowEV3.1.2TheTSACmightuseatleast0:9mmthicksteellayerasthegroundedlayer.
EV3.1.2
Electricallyconductiveseat,driverharness,and\002rewallmountingpointsaswellasthealuminiumlayeroftheTS\002rewall,seeT4.8,andtheLVSgroundmeasuringpoint,seeEV4.7.8,mustbegrounded,seeEV3.1.3.
EV3.1.3
AnelectricallyconductivepartisgroundedifitsresistancetoLVSgroundisbelow100mW,measuredwithacurrentof1A,andthegroundingwireisabletocontinuouslycarryatleast10%oftheTSaccumulatormainfusecurrentrating.
EV3.1.4
Partsofthevehiclewhichareormaybecomeelectricallyconductivewithin100mmofanyTScomponentmusthavearesistancebelow100WtoLVSground.
EV3.1.5
Therotatingpartofthewheelsdoesnotneedtobegrounded.
EV3.2OvercurrentProtection
EV3.2.1
Allelectricsystemsmusthaveappropriateovercurrentprotection.
EV3.2.2
Thecontinuouscurrentratingoftheovercurrentprotectionmustnotbegreaterthanthecontinuouscurrentratingofanyelectriccomponent,forexample,wire,busbar,orotherconductorsthatitprotects.I.e.ifmultiplepinsofaconnectorareusedtocarrycurrentsinparallel,eachpinmustbeappropriatelyprotected.
EV3.2.3
Allusedfusesmusthaveaninterruptcurrentratingthatishigherthanthetheoreticalshortcircuitcurrentofthesystemthatitprotects.
EV3.2.4
Allovercurrentprotectiondevicesmustberatedforthehighestvoltageinthesystemstheyprotect.AlldevicesusedmustberatedforDC.
EV3.2.5
AllovercurrentprotectiondevicesthatarepartoftheTSmustnotrelyonprogrammablelogic.Theovercurrentprotectionfunctionofmotorcontrollers/invertersforthemotoroutputsmayrelyonprogrammablelogic.

EV4TRACTIVESYSTEM
EV4.1GeneralRequirements
EV4.1.1
Themaximumallowedvoltagethatmayoccurbetweenanytwoelectricconnectionsis600VDCandformotorcontrollerandAMSinternallowpowercontrolsignals630VDC.
EV4.1.2
AllcomponentsintheTSmustberatedforthemaximumTSvoltage.TheTSareaofaPCB,seeEV4.3.6,isconsideredasonecomponent.EveryinputconnectedtotheTSmustberatedtothemaximumTSvoltage.

Allcomponentsmustberatedforthemaximumpossibletemperaturethatmayoccurduringusage.

EV4.2.1

EV4.3.1
TheentireTSandLVSmustbegalvanicallyisolated,seeEV1.2.1andIN4.1.1.

EV4.3.4

EV4.3.5
Componentsandcablescapableofmovementmustbepositivelyrestrainedtomaintainspacing.
EV4.3.6
IfTSandLVSareonthesamePCB,theymustbeonseparatewell-de\002nedareasoftheboard,meetingthespacingrequirementsintable5,eachareaclearlymarkedwith\223TS\224or\223LV\224.Theoutlineofthearearequiredforspacingmustbemarked.Grovesandcut-outsmusthaveaminimumwidthof1:5mmtoin\003uencethecreepagepath.\223Conformalcoating\224referstoacoatinginsulatoronaPCB.Solderresistisnotacoating.
EV4.3.7
Teamsmustbepreparedtodemonstratespacingonteam-builtequipment.Forinaccessiblecircuitry,fullyassembledspareboardsmustbeavailable.

EV6.1ShutdownCircuit\050SDC\051
Figure20
:
ExplanatoryexampleschematicoftherequiredShutdownCircuit\050SDC\051
EV6.1.1
TheShutdownCircuit\050SDC\051directlycarriesthepowerdrivingtheAIRs,seeEV5.6,andthepre-chargecircuitry,seeEV5.7.
EV6.1.2
TheSDCisde\002nedasaseriesconnectionofatleasttwomasterswitches,threeshutdownbuttons,theBOTS,seeT6.2,theIMD,theinertiaswitch,seeT11.5,theBSPD,seeT11.6,allrequiredinterlocks,andtheAMS.AnexplanatoryschematicoftherequiredSDC,excludinganypossibleinterlockcircuitry,isshowninFigure20.
EV6.1.3

EV6.1.4

EV6.1.5

EV6.1.7

EV6ShutdownCircuitandSystems
EV6.1.8
ItmustbepossibletodemonstratethatallfeaturesoftheSDCfunctioncorrectly.Thisincludesallinterlocks.
EV6.1.9
EverysystemthatisrequiredtoorcanopentheSDCmusthaveitsown,non-programmable,powerstagetoachievethis.TherespectivepowerstagesmustbedesignedtobeabletocarrytheSDCcurrent,e.g.AIRinrushcurrents,andsuchthatafailurecannotresultinelectricpowerbeingfedbackintotheelectricSDC.
EV6.1.10
Theshutdownbuttons,theBOTS,theTSMS,andallinterlocksmustnotactthroughanypowerstage.
EV6.1.11
Allsignalsin\003uencingtheSDCareSCSs,seeT11.9.
EV6.2TractiveSystemMasterSwitch\050TSMS\051

EV6.2.2
TheTSMSmustbe\002ttedwitha\223lockout/tagout\224capabilitytopreventaccidentalactivationoftheTS.TheESOmustensurethatitislockedintheoffpositionwheneverworkisdoneonthevehicleornoESOispresent.

EV6.3.1

EV6.3.2
TheIMDmustbeaBenderA-ISOMETER\256iso-F1IR155-3203,-3204,-4203,or-4204,oraBenderISOMETER\256iso165C-1,iso175,orequivalentIMDapprovedforautomotiveuse.Equivalencymaybeapprovedbytheof\002cialsbasedonthefollowingcriteria:robustnesstovibration,operatingtemperaturerange,IPrating,availabilityofadirectoutput,aself-testfacility,andmustnotbepoweredbythesystemthatismonitored.

EV6.3.6
OneIMDchassisgroundmeasurementlinemustbeconnectedtothegroundedTSACortherespectivegroundedTSenclosureoftheIMD.Theotherchassisgroundmeasurementlinemustbeconnectedtothemainhoop.Eachconnectionmustuseaseparateconductor,ratedforatleastmaximumTSvoltage.AnopencircuitinanyofthesegroundmeasurementconnectionsmustresultinanopenedSDC.
EV6.3.7
IncaseofaninsulationfailureoranIMDfailure,theIMDmustopentheSDC.Thismustbedonewithoutthein\003uenceofanyprogrammablelogic.SeealsoEV6.1.6regardingthere-activationoftheTSafteraninsulationfault.

EV8.1TractiveSystemAccumulatorContainerHandCart
EV8.1.1

EV8.1.5

EV8.1.6
ThehandcartmustbeabletocarrytheloadoftheTSACs.
EV8.1.7

EV8.1.8

EV8.1.9

EV8.1.10

EV8.1.11
ThehandcartitselfmusthavealabelaccordingtoEV5.3.7onits\002rewallmaximum1:3mabovetheground.

IN9[EVONLY]RAINTEST
IN9.1RainTestGeneralDe\002nitions
IN9.1.1

IN9.2RainTestProcedure
IN9.2.1
Thevehiclemustbeinready-to-racecondition.AllcomponentsandconstructionsusedtoprotectthevehiclefromwaterduringtheRainTestmustbeusedduringtheentireevent.Anyfans\050ifapplicable\051mustbeswitchedon.
IN9.2.2

IN9.2.3

IN9.2.4

S3.5CostedCarbonizedBillofMaterial\050CCBOM\051
S3.5.1
Themanufacturingandassemblyprocesses,realisticcostsandgreenhousegasemissionsofallpartsfromoneortwosystemsoftheBOMmustbelistedintheCCBOM.
S3.5.2
For2026,aCCBOMforthe\223EngineandTractiveSystem\224mustbesubmitted.
S3.5.3
AdditionaltotheBOM,theCCBOMmust:
\225
Includetheactualassociatedmanufacturingprocessesforeachpartasusedfortheprototypevehicle.
\225
Includetooling\050e.g.weldingjigs,molds,patternsanddies\051.
\225
Includetheactualcostsofmaterials,fabrication,boughtpartsandassemblyforeachpartasusedfortheprototypevehicle.
\225
Includethesystem'scarbonfootprintforeachpart,manufacturingandassemblyprocessoftheprototypevehicleinCO2einkg.
S3.5.4
Eachconstituent\223part\224ofan\223assembly\224mustbeclassi\002edas\223bought\224or\223made\224.
S3.5.5
AdditionaltothestructureoftheBOM,intheCCBOMeach\223part\224isbrokendowninto
\225
\223materials\224used\050e.g.aluminium\051
\225
\223processes\224thatdescribethenecessarystepsformanufacturing
\225
\223fasteners\224usedtoassemblethepart\050ifapplicable\051
\225
\223tooling\224requiredformanufacturing\050ifapplicable\051
\225
\223overhead\224foradditionalassociatedcostsandemissionsofmanufacturingandassem-blyofapartthatarenotcoveredbythepointsabove
S3.5.6
\223Tooling\224isthenecessarytoolsusedfortransformingthe\223material\224intothedesiredshape.Ifproductiontoolingisassociatedwithprocessesthatarespeci\002ctothepartgeometry,itmustbeincluded.Forexamplethediestostampoutachassisbracketaretooling.
S3.5.7
Handorpowertoolsmustnotbeincluded.
S3.5.8
For\223boughtparts\224,onlyfastenershavetobeincludedintheemissionscalculation\050ifapplicable\051.Fastenersthemselvesarenotconsideredboughtparts.Ifthepartwasmodi\002ed,theassociated\223processes\224and\223tooling\224\050ifapplicable\051forthismodi\002cationmustalsobeincluded.
S3.5.9
Amodi\002edboughtpartisconsideredamakepart,wheretheboughtpartactsasthematerial.Associated\223processes\224,\223tooling\224,\223fasteners\224and\223overhead\224\050ifapplicable\051forthemodi\002-cationsmustbeincluded.However,the\223carbonfootprint\224oftheboughtpartitselfshouldnotbeconsidered.
S3.5.10
Onlypartsthatcanbeboughtfromacatalogmaybedesignatedas\223boughtparts\224.
S3.5.11
Thecostcalculationsmustbecompletedasrealisticallyaspossibleandexcluderesearch,developmentandcapitalexpendituresforrealestates\050e.g.plantordevelopmenthoursoftheteam\051.
S3.5.12
AllcostsmustbedisplayedinEUR.ForcalculatingthepricesinEURfromothercurrencies,theteammustprovidetheexchangeratesused.

TheconceptoftheEngineeringDesignistoevaluatethestudent'sengineeringprocessandeffortthatwentintothedesignofavehicle,meetingtheintentofthecompetition.
S4.1.2
Proprietarycomponentsandsystemsthatareincorporatedintothevehicledesignas\002nisheditemsarenotevaluatedasastudentdesignedunit,butareonlyassessedontheteam'sselectionandapplicationofthatunit.
S4.1.3
Anevaluationconcerningthecapabilityofthevehicletodriveautonomouslywillalsobepartofthisdiscipline.Therefore,allsystemsthatarerequiredtodriveautonomouslywillbeinvestigated.ThisalsoincludesadiscussionaboutthehardwareandthesoftwareusedintheAS.
S4.2TechnicalVehicleSystemDocumentation\050TVSD\051
S4.2.1
TheTVSDshouldprovidethejudgeswithastructuredoverviewofthevehicleandtheteam'sdesignapproach.Thelevelofdetailmustallowtheevaluationofdesigndecisions,engineeringmethods,andvalidationproceduresaccordingtotheengineeringcategorieslistedintable7.Anyinformationrequiredtoscope,explainorhighlightdesignfeatures,concepts,methodsorobjectivesthatdemonstratethevalueandperformanceofthevehicleshouldbeincludedattheteam'sdiscretion.
S4.2.2
AcompletedTVSDmustbesubmittedonlineviathecompetitionwebsite,notlaterthanthedeadlinespeci\002edintheEventHandbook.
S4.2.3
TheTVSDmustincludethreetechnicaldrawings\050norenderings\051showingthevehiclefromthefront,topandside.Eachdrawingmustcontaintheoverallvehicledimensions\050length,width,andheight\051clearlyannotatedatleastonce.
S4.2.4
TheTVSDwillbeusedtosorttheteamsintoappropriateEngineeringDesignqueuesandtosupporttheevaluationduringEngineeringDesign.
S4.2.5
AnyevidenceorsupplementarydocumentationreferencedintheTVSDmustbeavailableattheeventandpresentedtothejudgesuponrequest.

EngineeringDesignstartswiththesubmissionoftheTVSDanditsreview.

Attheevent,teamswillpresenttheirknowledgeandtheirvehicletothejudges,whichwillevaluatetheteam'sperformancefollowingthedesignobjectivesstatedinchapterS4.1.
